# Christian Zionism

Za kristjane, ki pripadajo sionističnim denominacijam v južni Afriki, glejte [sionistične cerkve](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Zionist_churches?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "sionistične cerkve") .

**++Krščanski sionizem je politična in verska ideologija, ki v [krščanskem kontekstu](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christianity_and_Judaism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Krščanstvo in judovstvo") zagovarja vrnitev judovskega [ljudstva](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Jews?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Judje") v [Sveto deželo](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Holy_Land?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Sveta dežela") . [\[ 1 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-e-Rea_2012-1)++** Prav tako trdi, da je bila [ustanovitev Izraela](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Founding_of_Israel?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Ustanovitev Izraela") leta 1948 v skladu s [svetopisemskimi prerokbami,](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Bible_prophecy?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Biblijske prerokbe") ki so bile posredovane skozi [Staro zavezo](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Old_Testament?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Stara zaveza") : da je ponovna vzpostavitev [judovske suverenosti v Levantu](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/History_of_the_Jews_and_Judaism_in_the_Land_of_Israel?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Zgodovina Judov in judovstva v deželi Izrael") – eshatološko » [zbiranje Izraela](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Gathering_of_Israel?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Zbiranje Izraela") « – predpogoj za [drugi prihod Jezusa Kristusa](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Second_Coming?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Drugi prihod") . [\[ 1 \] ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-e-Rea_2012-1)[\[ 2 \] ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-sharif-2)[\[ 3 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-weber-3) Izraz se je začel uporabljati sredi 20. stoletja namesto **krščanskega restavratorstva** , ko so se zagovorniki ideologije zbrali za [sionisti](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "sionizem") v podporo [judovski nacionalni domovini](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Homeland_for_the_Jewish_people?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Domovina judovskega ljudstva") . [\[ 1 \] ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-e-Rea_2012-1)[\[ 4 \] ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-4)[\[ 5 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-5)





Pričakovanje judovske obnove med kristjani je zakoreninjeno v angleški [puritanski](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Puritan?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Puritanski") misli 17. stoletja. [\[ 6 \] ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-Paas2020-6)[\[ 2 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-sharif-2) V tem kontekstu so se pojavili krščanski prosionistični ideali. [\[ 6 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-Paas2020-6) Sodobna izraelska zgodovinarka [Anita Shapira](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Anita_Shapira?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Anita Šapira") meni, da so angleški sionistični [evangeličanski protestanti](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Evangelicalism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Evangeličanstvo") "to idejo prenesli na judovske kroge" okoli 1840-ih. [\[ 7 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-shapira-7)

Krščanski sionizem sicer podpira množično vrnitev Judov v [deželo Izrael](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Land_of_Israel?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Dežela Izrael") , a hkrati zagovarja vzporedno idejo, da bi bilo treba povratnike spodbujati k [zavrnitvi judovstva in sprejetju krščanstva](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Conversion_of_the_Jews\_\\(future_event\\)?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Spreobrnitev Judov (prihodnji dogodek)") kot sredstva za izpolnitev [svetopisemskih prerokb](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Bible_prophecy?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Biblijske prerokbe") . [\[ 1 \] ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-e-Rea_2012-1)[\[ 8 \] ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-lewis-8)[\[ 9 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-com-mag-9) Ankete in akademske raziskave kažejo na trend razširjenega nezaupanja med Judi do motivov evangeličanskih protestantov, ki so hkrati spodbujali podporo [državi Izrael](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/State_of_Israel?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Država Izrael") in [evangelizirali Jude . ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Proselytization_and_counter-proselytization_of_Jews?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Prozelitizacija in protiprozelitizacija Judov")[\[ 1 \] ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-e-Rea_2012-1)[\[ 10 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-10)

## Zgodovina pred 20. stoletjem



### Izvor kalvinističnega milenializma

![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/d/de/Thomas_Brightman.jpg)

[Thomas Brightman](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Thomas_Brightman?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Thomas Brightman") , angleški puritanec, je leta 1615 objavil delo z naslovom *»Se bodo spet vrnili v Jeruzalem?«.* To je bilo eno najzgodnejših [restavratorskih](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Restoration_Movement?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Gibanje za obnovo") del.

Prvi val protestantskih voditeljev, vključno z [Martinom Luthrom](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Martin_Luther?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Martin Luther") in [Johnom Calvinom](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/John_Calvin?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Janez Kalvin") , ni omenjal nobenih posebnih [eshatoloških](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_eschatology?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Krščanska eshatologija") pogledov, ki bi vključevali vrnitev Judov v Palestino (spreobrnjenih v krščanstvo ali ne). [\[ 11 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-lovers-11) Na splošno je Luther upal, da se bodo Judje spreobrnili v njegovo obliko krščanstva, ko bo prekinil s [katoliško cerkvijo](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Catholic_Church?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Katoliška cerkev") , vendar [je kasneje ostro obsodil Jude](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/On_the_Jews_and_Their_Lies?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "O Judih in njihovih lažih") . Tako kot katoliška cerkev in [vzhodna pravoslavna cerkev](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Eastern_Orthodox_Church?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Vzhodna pravoslavna cerkev") sta tudi luteranska in [reformirana cerkev ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Calvinism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Calvinism")[krščansko](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Lutheranism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Luteranstvo") cerkev videli kot "duhovni Izrael" in zveste kristjane imeli za izključno " [Božje ljudstvo](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/People_of_God?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "People of God") " – tiste, s katerimi je Bog sklenil zavezo po [Jezusu Kristusu](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Jesus_Christ?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Jesus Christ") – pri čemer osebam judovskega porekla niso dodelili posebnih privilegijev ali vloge. (V poznejših časih se je to imenovalo [supersesionizem](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Supersessionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Supersessionism") .) [\[ 12 \] ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-AlmenMadden2023-12)[\[ 11 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-lovers-11)

Protestantski poudarek na *[sola scriptura](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Sola_scriptura?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Samo pismo")* in širša razširjenost Svetega [pisma](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Bible?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Biblija") po Evropi v ljudskih jezikih pa sta različnim [radikalnim protestantom](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Radical_Reformation?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Radikalna reformacija") omogočila , da so Sveto pismo razlagali po svoje, na način, ki ni v celoti odražal niti srednjeveške [katoliške tradicije](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Catholic_tradition?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Katoliška tradicija") niti stališč samih voditeljev [protestantskega magistrata . ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Magisterial_Protestant?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Magisterijalni protestant")[\[ 11 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-lovers-11) S tem je prišlo do splošnega kulturnega [hebreizma med bolj radikalnimi protestanti, saj so ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Hebraism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "hebraizem")[čaščenje svetnikov](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Calendar_of_saints?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Koledar svetnikov") videli kot [malikovanje](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Idolatry?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Malikovanje") in so se bolj osredotočali na [svetopisemske patriarhe](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Patriarchs\_\\(Bible\\)?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Patriarhi (Biblija)") in [preroke](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Prophets_of_Christianity?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Preroki krščanstva") Stare [zaveze](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Old_Testament?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Stara zaveza") , pri čemer so svoje otroke pogosto poimenovali Abraham, Kajn, Jeremija, Zaharija, Daniel, Sampson in podobno. [\[ 13 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-13)

Pričakovanje, da se bodo Judje vrnili v [Palestino](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Palestine\_\\(region\\)?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Palestina (regija)") in jo spremenili v svojo nacionalno domovino, se je prvič pojavilo med skupinami, ki so se identificirale kot krščanske, v osemdesetih letih 16. stoletja, zlasti tistimi, ki so bile povezane s [puritanizmom](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Puritanism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Puritanizem") , reformirano vejo krščanstva, iz katere je nastala [kongregacionalistična](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Congregationalist?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Kongregacionalist") denominacija. [\[ 6 \] ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-Paas2020-6)[\[ 14 \] ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-pur-14)[\[ 11 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-lovers-11) Medtem ko je bil [Edvard VI. Angleški](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Edward_VI_of_England?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Edvard VI. iz Anglije") tudorski otrok-monarh [Anglije , je ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/England?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Anglija")*de facto* vladala kalvinistično usmerjena regentska oblast . To je kontinentalnim protestantom, kot sta [Martin Bucer](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Martin_Bucer?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Martin Bucer") in [Peter Martyr Vermigli,](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Peter_Martyr_Vermigli?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Peter mučenik Vermigli") omogočilo , da so poučevali na prestižnih univerzah v Cambridgeu in Oxfordu. [\[ 11 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-lovers-11) Ta dva moža sta predstavila [biblijsko eksegezo](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Biblical_exegesis?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Biblijska eksegeza") , ki je vključevala pomembno vlogo Judov, spreobrnjenih v krščanstvo, v [poslednjih časih](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Eschatology?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Eshatologija") . [\[ 11 \] ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-lovers-11)[\[ opomba 1 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-15) Zgodnje različice Svetega pisma, ki sta jih potrdili angleška monarhija in [anglikanska cerkev,](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Anglican_Church?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Anglikanska cerkev") so vključevale *[Veliko Biblijo](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Great_Bible?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Velika Biblija")* in *[Škofovo Biblijo](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Bishops%27_Bible?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Škofovska Biblija")* . Vendar pa so številni angleški [puritanci](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Puritans?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Puritanci") in [škotski ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Scottish_people?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Škoti")[prezbiterijanci](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Presbyterians?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Prezbiterijanci") iz nižin na Škotskem (skupaj z episkopalizmom in ustanovo " [protestantizma knezov](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Magisterial_Reformation?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Magisterijalna reformacija") ") na splošno menili, da so preveč " [romanistični](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Romanism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "romanizem") ". V odgovor so številni od teh puritancev in prezbiterijanov v šestdesetih letih 16. stoletja pod vodstvom Calvinovega naslednika [Theodora Beze nekaj časa preživeli v ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Theodore_Beza?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Teodor Beza")[Ženevi](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Geneva?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Ženeva") in ​​razvili prevod Svetega pisma, imenovan *[Ženevska Biblija](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Geneva_Bible?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Ženevska Biblija")* , ki je vseboval opombe v zvezi s Pismom *[Rimljanom](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Book_of_Romans?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Knjiga Rimljanom")* , v katerih je posebej trdil, da se bodo Judje v poslednjih časih spreobrnili v krščanstvo, in preusmeril pozornost na Palestino kot osrednje prizorišče. [\[ 11 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-lovers-11) To stališče so močno prevzeli angleški puritanci (kot so [Francis Kett](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Francis_Kett?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Francis Kett") , [Edmund Bunny](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Edmund_Bunny?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Edmund Bunny") , [Thomas Draxe](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Thomas_Draxe?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Thomas Draxe") , [Thomas Brightman](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Thomas_Brightman?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Thomas Brightman") , [Joseph Mede](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Joseph_Mede?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Jožef Mede") , [William Perkins](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/William_Perkins\_\\(theologian\\)?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "William Perkins (teolog)") , [Richard Sibbes](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Richard_Sibbes?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Richard Sibbes") , [Thomas Goodwin](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Thomas_Goodwin?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Thomas Goodwin") , [William Strong](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/William_Strong\_\\(priest,\_died_1654\\)?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "William Strong (duhovnik, umrl 1654)") , [William Bridge](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/William_Bridge?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Viljemov most") , [Henry Finch](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Henry_Finch\_\\(died_1625\\)?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Henry Finch (umrl 1625)") , [John Owen](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/John_Owen\_\\(theologian\\)?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "John Owen (teolog)") in [Giles Fletcher](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Giles_Fletcher?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Giles Fletcher") ), škotski prezbiterijanci iz nižin na Škotskem (kot so[George Gillespie](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/George_Gillespie?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "George Gillespie") , [Robert Baillie](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Robert_Baillie?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Robert Baillie") in [Samuel Rutherford](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Samuel_Rutherford?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Samuel Rutherford") ), [\[ 15 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-rutherford-16) in celo nekateri celinski protestanti (kot so [Oliger Paulli](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Oliger_Paulli?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Oliger Paulli") , [Isaac Vossius](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Isaac_Vossius?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Isaac Vossius") , [Hugo Grotius](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Hugo_Grotius?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Hugo Grotius") , [Gerhard Vossius](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Gerhard_Vossius?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Gerhard Vossius") in [David Blondel](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/David_Blondel?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "David Blondel") ). [\[ 11 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-lovers-11)

![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/2/24/Oliver_Cromwell_by_Samuel_Cooper.jpg/250px-Oliver_Cromwell_by_Samuel_Cooper.jpg)

 Puritanci, nekoč "obrobna" frakcija, so pod [Oliverjem Cromwellom](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Oliver_Cromwell?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Oliver Cromwell") prišli na oblast v času Commonwealtha. Več njegovih najbližjih svetovalcev je imelo filosemitske milenijalistične verske poglede.

V poznem obdobju Tudorjev in zgodnjih Stuartov so ti puritanci ostali tujci v Angliji in so ostro nasprotovali anglikanski cerkvi, v kateri so prevladovali [Laudijci](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Laudian?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Laudian") (čeprav so prezbiterijanci, ki so imeli zelo podobne poglede, ustanovili [Škotsko cerkev](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Church_of_Scotland?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Škotska cerkev") kot največjo " [cerkev](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Kirk\_\\(word\\)?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Kirk (beseda)") " na Škotskem). Z [angleško državljansko vojno](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/English_Civil_War?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Angleška državljanska vojna") so puritanci zapolnili vrste parlamentarcev [in](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Roundheads?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Okrogle glave") Nove [modelne armade](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/New_Model_Army?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Nova modelna vojska") . Pod vodstvom [Oliverja Cromwella](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Oliver_Cromwell?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Oliver Cromwell") so zmagali, usmrtili [Karla I. Angleškega](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Charles_I_of_England?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Karel I. Angleški") in pridobili popolno državno oblast, s čimer so med letoma 1649 in 1660 ustanovili [Angleško zvezo . ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Commonwealth_of_England?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Angleška zveza")[\[ 16 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-cz-17) Filo [\-semitska ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Philo-Semitic?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Filo-semitski")[milenijska](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Millennialist?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Milenijalist") podtalnica je začela neposredno vplivati ​​na politiko. Številni Cromwellovi tesni svetovalci, kot so [John Dury](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/John_Dury?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Janez Dury") , [John Sadler](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/John_Sadler\_\\(Town_Clerk_of_London\\)?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "John Sadler (mestni uradnik Londona)") in [Hugh Peter](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Hugh_Peter?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Hugh Peter") , so prišli v stik z [Judi s sedežem na Nizozemskem,](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/History_of_the_Jews_in_the_Netherlands?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Zgodovina Judov na Nizozemskem") kot je bil [Menasseh ben Israel](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Menasseh_ben_Israel?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Menasseh ben Israel") , in se zavzemali [za preselitev Judov v Anglijo](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Resettlement_of_the_Jews_in_England?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Preselitev Judov v Anglijo") (v državi so jim bili prepovedani že od 13. stoletja). Sadler, Cromwellov tajnik, je v svoji brošuri *Pravice kraljestva (1649) celo trdil, da so Britanci eno od* [izgubljenih izraelskih plemen](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Lost_Tribes_of_Israel?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Izgubljena izraelska plemena") in so zato sorodni Judom, kar je bil začetek [britanskega izraelizma](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/British_Israelism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Britanski izraelizem") . Drugi puritanci, kot so [Jeremiah Burroughs](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Jeremiah_Burroughs?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Jeremiah Burroughs") , [Peter Bulkley](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Peter_Bulkley?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Peter Bulkley") , [John Fenwicke](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/John_Fenwicke?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "John Fenwicke") in [John Cotton](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/John_Cotton\_\\(minister\\)?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "John Cotton (minister)") , [\[ 17 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-owen-18) nekateri od njih so živeli v [koloniji Massachusetts Bay](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Massachusetts_Bay_Colony?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Kolonija Massachusetts Bay") , so v ponovni vrnitve Judov v Anglijo videli korak na poti k njihovi morebitni vrnitvi v Palestino (vse to je bilo povezano z milenijsko eshatologijo, ki bi pospešila [drugi prihod](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Second_Coming?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Drugi prihod") Jezusa Kristusa in s tem [končno sodbo](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Final_judgement?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Končna sodba") ). [\[ 18 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-19) Johanna in Ebenezer Cartwright, dva baptista, ki sta nekaj časa preživela v [Amsterdamu](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Amsterdam?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Amsterdam") , sta imela enako stališče in januarja 1649 vložila prvotno peticijo za ponovni sprejem Judov pri vojnem svetu [Thomasa Fairfaxa : ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Thomas_Fairfax?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Thomas Fairfax")[\[ 19 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-20) peticija je upala: »Da bo ta angleški narod skupaj s prebivalci Nizozemske prvi in ​​najbolj pripravljen prepeljati izraelske sinove in hčere na svojih ladjah v deželo, obljubljeno njihovim prednikom, Abrahamu, Izaku in Jakobu, za večno dediščino.« [\[ 16\]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-cz-17)Njihova*dejanska*[ toleranca v Angliji](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-cz-17)[ je bila neformalno dosežena med letoma 1655 in 1656 in po restavraciji](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Stuart_Restoration?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Stuartova restavracija")ni bila odpravljena.

Pomemben prednik 17. stoletja , ki je bil nominalno [hugenotsko-](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Huguenot?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Hugenot") kalvinistični, a je izhajal iz portugalske [novokrščanske](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/New_Christian?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Novi kristjan") (spreobrnjene [sefardske judovske](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Sephardic_Jewish?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Sefardski Judje") ) družine, je bil tudi pomemben prednik 17. stoletja, z vplivom na obeh straneh Rokavskega [preliva](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/English_Channel?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Rokavski preliv") . [\[ 20 \] ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-21)[\[ 16 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-cz-17)[ La Peyrère](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Isaac_La_Peyr%C3%A8re?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Isaac La Peyrère") je v svojem milenijalističnem delu *Du rappel des juifs* (1643) pisal o vrnitvi Judov v Palestino in napovedal, da bosta [gradnja tretjega templja](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Third_Temple?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Tretji tempelj") in [Jeruzalema](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Jerusalem?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Jeruzalem") igrala najmočnejšo vlogo v svetovnem upravljanju: vse to je delovalo v smeri drugega prihoda. [\[ 16 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-cz-17) La Peyrère je pozorno spremljal razvoj disidentskega režima Oliverja Cromwella in sanjal o strmoglavljenju [francoskega kralja Ludvika XIV.](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Louis_XIV_of_France?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Ludvik XIV. iz Francije") in njegovi zamenjavi s [princem Condéjem](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Louis,\_Grand_Cond%C3%A9?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Louis, Veliki grof") (za katerega je delal kot tajnik) kot del milenialističnega protosionističnega mesijanskega projekta. [\[ 21 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-22) Po objavi La Peyrèrove knjige je Menasseh Ben Israel, ki je živel v Amsterdamu, obvestil svojega prijatelja [Petrusa Serrariusa](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Petrus_Serrarius?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Petrus Serrarius") (tesnega sodelavca Johna Duryja) o pomenu teorij, ki so pokazale zgodnjo interakcijo med judovskim in protestantskim protosionizmom v 17. stoletju. [\[ 22 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-pop-23) Drugi celinski protestantski milenijski privrženci, ki so jih navduševale La Peyrèrove teorije, so bili Nemca [Abraham von Franckenberg](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Abraham_von_Franckenberg?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Abraham von Franckenberg") (študent kabale [)](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Kabbalah?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Kabala") in Paul Felgenhauer. [\[ 22 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-pop-23) Menasseh Ben Israel je leta 1652 sam napisal delo *Upanje Izraela.* Serrarius je med protestanti v Amsterdamu postal glavni podpornik sporočila, da je [Sabbatai Zevi ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Sabbatai_Zevi?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Sabbatai Zevi")[Mesija](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Messiah?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Mesija") , kot ga je oznanil [Nathan iz Gaze](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Nathan_of_Gaza?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Natan iz Gaze") (njegovi privrženci, [sabatejci](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Sabbateans?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Sabatejci") , so imeli sedež v [Otomanskem cesarstvu](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Ottoman_Empire?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Osmansko cesarstvo") , vendar je imel pomembno podporo v celotni [judovski diaspori](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Jewish_diaspora?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Judovska diaspora") ). [\[ 23 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-Nadler_2007-24)

Čeprav so bili milenijski [puritanci, ki so se preselili v Novo Anglijo](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Puritan_migration_to_New_England\_\\(1620%E2%80%931640\\)?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Puritanska selitev v Novo Anglijo (1620–1640)") , odstranjeni z oblasti v sami Angliji, so še naprej imeli globljo kulturno zapuščino v družbi. Poleg Johna Cottona je bil tudi [Increase Mather](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Increase_Mather?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Povečaj Mather") , eden prvih predsednikov [Harvard Collegea,](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Harvard_University?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Univerza Harvard") močan zagovornik vrnitve Judov v Palestino. [\[ 14 \] ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-pur-14)[\[ 16 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-cz-17) Je avtor številnih del, med katerimi je najbolj znano njegovo delo *Skrivnost Izraelovega odrešenja* (1669). [\[ 16 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-cz-17) [Rogerja Williamsa](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Roger_Williams?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Roger Williams") , puritanskega zagovornika [verske svobode](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Religious_liberty?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Verska svoboda") (tudi za Jude) [\[ 24 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-25) v [koloniji Rhode Island , ki jo je ustanovil, so v govorih kasnejših judovskih sionističnih voditeljev, kot je ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Colony_of_Rhode_Island_and_Providence_Plantations?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Kolonija Rhode Island in plantaže Providence")[Stephen S. Wise](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Stephen_S.\_Wise?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Stephen S. Wise") , označili za protosionista zaradi njegove pripombe: »Hrepenel sem po trgovanju z Judi samimi, za čigar strogo mero se bojim, da narodi in Anglija še nimajo računa.« [\[ 25 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-26) Nekateri pomembni [filozofi 17. stoletja](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/17th-century_philosophy?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Filozofija 17. stoletja") , ki so delovali kot most med milenijskimi sektaši svojega časa in bližajočo se [dobo razsvetljenstva](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Age_of_the_Enlightenment?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Doba razsvetljenstva") z njeno [znanstveno revolucijo,](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Scientific_Revolution?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Znanstvena revolucija") so imeli bodisi stališča, povezana s premilenialnimi restavratorji bodisi so se gibali tesno v njihovih krogih: to še posebej velja za sira [Isaaca Newtona](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Isaac_Newton?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Isaac Newton") in [Barucha Spinozo](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Baruch_Spinoza?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Baruh Spinoza") . Še posebej Newton, ki je imel stališča [radikalne reformacije ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Radical_Reformation?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Radikalna reformacija")[glede religije](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Religious_views_of_Isaac_Newton?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Verski pogledi Isaaca Newtona") in [se ukvarjal tudi z okultizmom](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Isaac_Newton%27s_occult_studies?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Isaac Newtonove okultne študije") (vključno s kabalo), je napovedal vrnitev Judov v Palestino, obnovo Jeruzalema konec 19. stoletja in postavitev tretjega templja v 20. ali 21. stoletju, kar bi vodilo do konca sveta najkasneje leta 2060. [\[ 26 \] ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-27)[\[ 27 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-newton-28) Veliko teh zasebnih spisov je bilo za njegove podpornike, ki so ga želeli podpreti kot človeka razuma in znanosti v nasprotju z [Leibnizom](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Gottfried_Wilhelm_Leibniz?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Gottfried Wilhelm Leibniz") , in čeprav je [Univerza v Cambridgeu](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/University_of_Cambridge?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Univerza v Cambridgeu") podedovala njegove znanstvene članke, teh zasebnih ni hotela sprejeti. [\[ 27 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-newton-28) Mnoge od teh del, ki jih je zbral [Abraham Yahuda](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Abraham_Yahuda?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Abraham Jahuda") , so od leta 1967 shranjene v [Izraelski nacionalni knjižnici. ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/National_Library_of_Israel?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Narodna knjižnica Izraela")[\[ 27 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-newton-28) Spinoza se je, čeprav je bil sam Jud, na Nizozemskem gibal v krogih, med katerimi so bili tudi Petrus Serrarius,[Henry Oldenburg](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Henry_Oldenburg?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Henry Oldenburg") in je bil celo neposredno pod vplivom La Peyrèra. [\[ 28 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-29)



### Pietizem, evangeličanstvo in britanska zunanja politika

Z vzponom hanoverjevcev [na](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/House_of_Hanover?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Hiša Hannover") oblast v Veliki Britaniji in vzponom razsvetljenstva je velik del prevladujoče elite 18. stoletja sprejel [filhelenizem](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Philhellenism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Filhelenizem") in se za navdih za [gruzijsko dobo](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Georgian_architecture?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Gruzijska arhitektura") oziral nazaj v kulturo in filozofije klasičnega [sveta](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Classical_world?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Klasični svet") , namesto da bi se ukvarjal z milenijskimi fantazijami, ki so temeljile na hebrejski Stari zavezi (čeprav so Judje sami v [Britanskem imperiju](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/British_Empire?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Britanski imperij") uživali precejšnjo toleranco ). Čeprav je bilo sprva marginalno, se je od tridesetih let 18. stoletja počasi razvijalo versko podzemlje, ki je sčasoma sprožilo drugi val protestantskega sionizma in z njim rojstvo [evangeličanskega protestantizma](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Evangelical_Protestantism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Evangeličanski protestantizem") . \[ *[potreben citat](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Wikipedija:Potreben je navedni vir")* \] To je v Nemčiji sprožil [Philipp Spener](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Philipp_Spener?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Philipp Spener") s svojim [pietizmom](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Pietism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Pietizem") , mističnim in pogosto milenijskim pristopom k luteranstvu, ki je prerokoval »spreobrnitev Judov in padec papeštva [kot](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Papacy?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Papeštvo") uvod v zmagoslavje Cerkve«. Eden od Spenerjevih privržencev, [Nicolaus Zinzendorf](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Nicolaus_Zinzendorf?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Nicolaus Zinzendorf") , je to razširil v [moravsko cerkev](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Moravian_Church?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Moravska cerkev") in teorijo povezal s Palestino ter spremenil moravsko liturgijo, da bi vključila molitev »za obnovitev Judovega plemena v njegovem času in blagoslov njegovih prvih sadov med nami«. [\[ 16 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-cz-17)[ John](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/John_Wesley?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "John Wesley") in [Charles Wesley](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Charles_Wesley?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Charles Wesley") , zgodnja voditelja [metodizma](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Methodism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Metodizem") , ki sta se navdihovala pri pietistih in Zinzendorfovih moravskih vernikih, sta prav tako spodbujala vrnitev Judov v Palestino, Charles Wesley pa je celo napisal himno, posvečeno tej temi. [\[ 16 \] ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-cz-17)[\[ 29 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-wes-30) Baptist [John Gill](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/John_Gill\_\\(theologian\\)?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "John Gill (teolog)") , ki se je gibal v podobnih krogih kot Wesleyjevi, je napisal dela, ki so izražala podobna stališča. [\[ 30 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-gill-31) Do leta 1771 je evangeličanski duhovnik [John Eyre](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/John_Eyre\_\\(evangelical_minister\\)?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "John Eyre (evangeličanski duhovnik)") , ustanovitelj revije *[Evangelical Magazine](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Evangelical_Magazine?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Evangeličanska revija")* in eden od prvotnih članov Londonskega *[misijonarskega društva,](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/London_Missionary_Society?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Londonsko misijonarsko društvo")* promoviral bolj razvito različico teh stališč s svojim delom *Opažanja o prerokbah v zvezi z obnovo Judov* . [\[ 16 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-cz-17)

Do konca 18. stoletja, po [francoski revoluciji](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/French_Revolution?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Francoska revolucija") in odloku [Narodne skupščine](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/National_Assembly\_\\(French_Revolution\\)?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Državni zbor (francoska revolucija)") decembra 1789, ki je omogočil, da so nekatoličani upravičeni do vseh civilnih in vojaških položajev, je revolucionarna vlada v Franciji izkoristila priložnost [za zvestobo Judov](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Napoleon_and_the_Jews?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Napoleon in Judje") , s čimer je konkurirala Veliki Britaniji. Med [egiptovsko-sirsko kampanjo](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/French_campaign_in_Egypt_and_Syria?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Francoska kampanja v Egiptu in Siriji") francoskih [revolucionarnih vojn](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/French_Revolutionary_Wars?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Francoske revolucionarne vojne") je [Bonaparte](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Napoleon?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Napoleon") povabil "vse Jude Azije in Afrike, naj se zberejo pod njegovo zastavo, da bi ponovno vzpostavili starodavni Jeruzalem". [\[ 31 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-32) Čeprav je bil Bonaparte sam sekularen in je bila ideja zgodnji primer pragmatičnega [političnega sionizma](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Political_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Politični sionizem") , je sama jakobinska ideja morda izvirala od [Thomasa Corbeta](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Corbet_family?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Družina Corbet") (1773–1804), [anglo-irskega](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Anglo-Irish_people?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Anglo-irski ljudje") protestantskega emigranta, ki je bil kot član liberalno-republikanskega [Društva združenih Ircev](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Society_of_United_Irishmen?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Društvo združenih Ircev") zaveznik [jakobinske](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Jacobin?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Jakobinec") vlade, se je ukvarjal z revolucionarnimi dejavnostmi proti Britancem in služil v francoski vojski. [\[ 32 \] ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-corb-33)[\[ 33 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-dem-34) Februarja 1799 je napisal pismo francoskemu [imeniku](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/French_Directory?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Francoski imenik") , ki je bil takrat pod vodstvom Napoleonovega mecena [Paula Barrasa](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Paul_Barras?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Paul Barras") . [\[ 32 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-corb-33) V pismu je zapisal: »Napoleon, priporočam ti, da pozoveš judovsko ljudstvo, naj se pridruži tvojemu osvajanju na Vzhodu, tvojemu poslanstvu osvojitve dežele Izrael,« rekoč: »Njihovo bogastvo jih ne tolaži v stiskah. Z nestrpnostjo čakajo na obdobje svoje ponovne vzpostavitve kot naroda.« [\[ 33 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-dem-34) Milka Levy-Rubin, kustosinja v [Izraelski nacionalni knjižnici](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/National_Library_of_Israel?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Narodna knjižnica Izraela") , je Corbetovo motivacijo pripisala protestantskemu sionizmu, ki je temeljil na premilenialističnih temah. [\[ 32 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-corb-33)

V Novi Angliji je bil v 18. stoletju [Ezra Stiles](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Ezra_Stiles?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Ezra Stiles") , predsednik [kolidža Yale](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Yale_University?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Univerza Yale") , podpornik obnove Judov in se je leta 1773 med svojim obiskom spoprijateljil z rabinom [Raphaelom Chaimom Yitzchakom Karigalom](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Raphael_Hayyim_Isaac_Carregal?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Raphael Hayyim Isaac Carregal") iz [Hebrona . ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Hebron?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Hebron")[\[ 34 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-35) [Tudi Jonathan Edwards](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Jonathan_Edwards\_\\(theologian\\)?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Jonathan Edwards (teolog)") je pričakoval prihodnjo vrnitev Judov v domovino. [\[ 35 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-Stein-36) Leta 1808 je prezbiterijanec [Asa McFarland](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/w/index.php?title=Asa_McFarland&action=edit&redlink=1&\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Asa McFarland (stran ne obstaja)") izrazil mnenje mnogih, da je padec Osmanskega cesarstva neizbežen in da bo prinesel obnovo Judov. \[ *[potreben citat](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Wikipedija:Potreben je navedni vir")* \] Neki David Austin iz [New Havna](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/New_Haven,\_Connecticut?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "New Haven, Connecticut") je svoje bogastvo porabil za gradnjo pristanišč in gostiln, iz katerih bi se Judje lahko vkrcali v Sveto deželo. \[ *[potreben citat](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Wikipedija:Potreben je navedni vir")* \] Leta 1825 [je Mordecai Manuel Noah](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Mordecai_Manuel_Noah?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Mordekaj Manuel Noah") , Jud, ki je želel ustanoviti nacionalni dom za Jude na [Grand Islandu](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Grand_Island,\_New_York?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Veliki otok, New York") v New Yorku kot vmesno postajo na poti v Sveto deželo, za svoj projekt pridobil široko krščansko podporo. \[ *[potreben citat](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Wikipedija:Potreben je navedni vir")* \] Prav tako je bila restavratorska teologija med navdihi za prvo ameriško misijonarsko dejavnost na Bližnjem vzhodu [\[ 36 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-37) in za kartiranje Svete dežele. [\[ 37 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-38)

Večina britanskih restavratorjev iz začetka 19. stoletja, kot je bil [Charles Simeon](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Charles_Simeon?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Karel Simeon") , je bila v eshatologiji [postmilenijcev . ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Postmillennialism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Postmilenializem")[\[ 8 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-lewis-8) Z vzponom [Jamesa Frereja](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/James_Hatley_Frere?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "James Hatley Frere") , [Jamesa Haldana Stewarta](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/James_Haldane_Stewart?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "James Haldane Stewart") in [Edwarda Irvinga](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Edward_Irving?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Edward Irving") se je v dvajsetih letih 19. stoletja zgodil velik premik k [premilenializmu](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Premillennialism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Premilenializem") , s podobnim poudarkom na zagovarjanju vrnitve Judov v Izrael. [\[ 8 \] ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-lewis-8)[\[ 38 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-39) Ko se je zdelo, da se bliža propad Osmanskega cesarstva, se je zagovarjanje restavratorstva povečalo. \[ *[potreben citat](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Wikipedija:Potreben je navedni vir")* \]

![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/a/a2/Anthony_Ashley-Cooper%2C_7th_Earl_of_Shaftesbury_by_John_Collier.jpg/250px-Anthony_Ashley-Cooper%2C_7th_Earl_of_Shaftesbury_by_John_Collier.jpg)

 Grof [Shaftesburyjski](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Anthony_Ashley-Cooper,\_7th_Earl_of_Shaftesbury?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Anthony Ashley-Cooper, 7. grof Shaftesburyjski") , na katerega so vplivali [evangeličanski anglikanizem](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Evangelical_Anglicanism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Evangeličanski anglikanizem") in stališča [Edwarda Bickerstetha,](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Edward_Bickersteth\_\\(priest\\)?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Edward Bickersteth (duhovnik)") je bil eden prvih britanskih politikov, ki je resno podprl vrnitev Judov v [osmansko Palestino](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Ottoman_Syria?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Osmanska Sirija") kot uradno politiko.

Razpad Osmanskega cesarstva je ogrozil britansko pot v Indijo prek [Sueškega prekopa](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Suez_Canal?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Sueški prekop") , pa tudi različne francoske, nemške in ameriške gospodarske interese. Leta 1831 je ekspanzionistični Egipt v [prvi turško-egiptovski vojni pregnal Osmane iz ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/First_Turko-Egyptian_War?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Prva turško-egipčanska vojna")[regije Sirije](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Syria\_\\(region\\)?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Sirija (regija)") (vključno s Palestino) . Čeprav je Britanija prisilila [Mohameda Alija](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Muhammad_Ali_of_Egypt?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Muhammad Ali iz Egipta") , da se umakne v Egipt, je [Levant](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/The_Levant?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Levant") za kratek čas ostal brez vlade. Zaradi nenehne šibkosti Osmanskega cesarstva so nekateri na Zahodu razmišljali o potencialu judovske države v Sveti deželi. Številne pomembne osebnosti v britanski vladi so zagovarjale tak načrt, vključno s [Charlesom Henryjem Churchillom](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Charles_Henry_Churchill?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Charles Henry Churchill") . [\[ 39 \] ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-40)[\[ 40 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-41) Tudi v času pred [krimsko vojno](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Crimean_War?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Krimska vojna") (1854) se je ponudila priložnost za politične prerazporeditve na Bližnjem vzhodu. Julija 1853 je [Anthony Ashley-Cooper, 7. grof Shaftesburyjski](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Anthony_Ashley-Cooper,\_7th_Earl_of_Shaftesbury?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Anthony Ashley-Cooper, 7. grof Shaftesburyjski") , ki je bil predsednik [Londonskega društva za promocijo krščanstva med Judi](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Church%27s_Ministry_Among_Jewish_People?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Cerkveno delo med Judi") , pisal [premierju Aberdeenu](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/George_Hamilton-Gordon,\_4th_Earl_of_Aberdeen?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "George Hamilton-Gordon, 4. grof Aberdeenski") in pozval k obnovi judovskega prebivališča kot načinu stabilizacije regije. [\[ 8 \] ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-lewis-8)[\[ 41 \] ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-42)[\[ 42 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-43)

Hkrati je obisk [Johna Nelsona Darbyja](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/John_Nelson_Darby?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "John Nelson Darby") v Združenih državah Amerike \[ *[kdaj?](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Wikipedia:Manual_of_Style/Dates_and_numbers?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#Chronological_items "Wikipedija:Priročnik sloga/Datumi in številke")* \] sprožil novo gibanje. \[ *[potreben citat](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Wikipedija:Potreben je navedni vir")* \] Darby je bil ustanovitelj teološkega okvira, znanega kot [dispenzacionalizem](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Dispensationalism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Dispenzacionalizem") . Darbyjeva dispenzacionalistična teologija se pogosto smatra za pomembnega prebuditelja ameriškega krščanskega sionizma. [\[ 43 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-Kuttab-44) Prvi je razlikoval med upi Judov in upi cerkve ter poganov v seriji 11 večernih predavanj v Ženevi leta 1840. Njegova predavanja so bila takoj objavljena v francoščini ( *L'Attente Actuelle de l'Eglise* ), angleščini (1841), nemščini in nizozemščini (1847) in tako so njegovi nauki začeli svojo globalno pot. Nekateri dispenzacionalisti, kot je [Arno Gabelein](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/w/index.php?title=Arno_Gabelein&action=edit&redlink=1&\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Arno Gabelein (stran ne obstaja)") , so bili sicer [filosemitski](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Philo-Semitism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Filosemitizem") , nasprotovali sionizmu kot gibanju, rojenemu iz samozavesti in nevere. [\[ 3 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-weber-3) Čeprav je imel dispenzacionalizem precejšen vpliv prek [Scofieldove referenčne Biblije](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Scofield_Reference_Bible?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Scofieldova referenčna Biblija") (prvič izdane pri založbi OUP leta 1909), [\[ 43 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-Kuttab-44) je krščansko lobiranje za obnovitev Judov pred njegovo objavo potekalo več kot stoletje, [\[ 8 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-lewis-8) in mnogi krščanski sionisti in krščansko-sionistične organizacije, kot je [Mednarodno krščansko veleposlaništvo Jeruzalem,](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/International_Christian_Embassy_Jerusalem?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Mednarodno krščansko veleposlaništvo Jeruzalem") se dispenzacionalizma ne strinjajo. \[ *[potreben vir](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Wikipedija:Potreben je navedni vir")* \] Mnogi nedispenzacionalistični protestanti so bili tudi odločni zagovorniki vrnitve Judov v domovino, [Charles Spurgeon](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Charles_Spurgeon?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Charles Spurgeon") , [\[ 44 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-restor-45) tako [Horatius ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Horatius_Bonar?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Horatius Bonar")[\[ 45 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-Bonar-46) kot [Andrew Bonar](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Andrew_Bonar?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Andrej Bonar") , \[ *[potreben vir](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Wikipedija:Potreben je navedni vir")* \] [Robert Murray M'Chyene](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Robert_Murray_M%27Cheyne?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Robert Murray M'Cheyne") , [\[ 46 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-MCheyne-47) \[ *[potrebna potrditev](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Wikipedija:Preverljivost")* \] in [JC Ryle ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/J.\_C.\_Ryle?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "JC Ryle")[\[ 47 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-ReferenceA-48) \[ *[potrebna potrditev](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Wikipedija:Preverljivost")* \] so bili med številnimi zagovorniki tako pomena kot pomembnosti vrnitve Judov v Izrael. Vendar je Spurgeon zatrjeval dispenzacionalizem: »Usmiljenje je, da se te absurde razkrivajo ena za drugo, da bi lahko prenašali njihovo neumnost, ne da bi umrli od začudenja.« [\[ 48 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-49) Leta 1864 je Spurgeon zapisal:

> Torej se veselimo teh dveh stvari. Ne bom teoretiziral o tem, katera od njiju bo prišla prej – ali bosta najprej obnovljeni in nato spreobrnjeni – ali pa se bosta najprej spreobrnili in nato obnovili. Obnovljeni bodo in tudi spreobrnjeni bodo. [\[ 44 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-restor-45)

Darbyjev dispenzacionalizem je bil izražen na [Niagarski biblijski konferenci](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Niagara_Bible_Conference?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Niagarska biblijska konferenca") leta 1878 \[ *[potreben citat](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Wikipedija:Potreben je navedni vir")* \] , ki je izdala 14-točkovno razglasitev (ki se je opirala na Luka 12:35–40, 17:26–30, 18:8, Apostolska dela 15:14–17, 2 Tesaloničanom 2:3–8, 2 Timoteju 3:1–5 in Titu 1:11–15), vključno z:

> da bo Gospod Jezus prišel osebno, da bi uvedel tisočletno dobo, ko bo Izrael vrnjen v svojo deželo in bo zemlja polna spoznanja o Gospodu; in da je ta osebni in predtisoletni prihod blagoslovljeno upanje, ki nam ga ponuja evangelij, na katerega bi morali nenehno čakati. [\[ 49 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-50)

Nemesijanski restavratorski sistem poznega 19. stoletja je bil v veliki meri posledica skrbi za usodo Judov v Ruskem imperiju, ki so ga pestile revščina in smrtonosni pogromi, ki jih je navdihovala vlada. Splošno sprejeto je bilo, da zahodne države niso želele sprejeti judovskih priseljencev. Restavratorski sistem je bil način, kako so dobrodelni posamezniki pomagali zatiranim Judom, ne da bi jih dejansko sprejeli kot sosede in someščane. [\[ 50 \] ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-51)[\[ 51 \] ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-52)[\[ 52 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-53) V tem se restavratorski sistem ni razlikoval od prizadevanj [Ameriškega kolonizacijskega društva](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/American_Colonization_Society?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Ameriško kolonizacijsko društvo") , da bi črnce poslali v [Liberijo,](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Liberia?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Liberija") in prizadevanj britanskih abolicionistov za ustanovitev [Sierre Leone](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Sierra_Leone?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Sierra Leone") . \[ *[potreben citat](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Wikipedija:Potreben je navedni vir")* \] [Winston Churchill](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Winston_Churchill?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Winston Churchill") je podpiral restavratorski sistem, ker je spoznal, da Judje, ki bežijo pred ruskimi pogromi, potrebujejo zatočišče, in je iz sentimentalnih razlogov imel raje Palestino. [\[ 53 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-54)



### V Združenih državah Amerike

![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/d/d5/Memorandum_to_Protestant_Monarchs_of_Europe_for_the_restoration_of_the_Jews_to_Palestine%2C_Colonial_Times_1841.jpg/250px-Memorandum_to_Protestant_Monarchs_of_Europe_for_the_restoration_of_the_Jews_to_Palestine%2C_Colonial_Times_1841.jpg)

 "Memorandum protestantskim silam severne Evrope in Amerike", *[Colonial Times](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Colonial_Times?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Kolonialni časi")* , Hobart, Avstralija, 1841

Leta 1818 je predsednik [John Adams](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/John_Adams?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Janez Adams") zapisal: »Resnično si želim, da bi Judje v Judeji spet postali neodvisen narod,« in verjel, da bodo postopoma postali [unitaristični](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Unitarianism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Unitarizem") kristjani. [\[ 54 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-55)

Leta 1844 je [George Bush](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/George_Bush\_\\(Biblical_scholar\\)?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "George Bush (biblijski strokovnjak)") , profesor hebrejščine na [Univerzi v New Yorku](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/New_York_University?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Univerza v New Yorku") in bratranec prednika predsednikov Busha, objavil knjigo z naslovom *Dolina vizij ali Oživljene suhe kosti Izraela* . V njej je obsodil »suženjstvo in zatiranje, ki jih (Jude) tako dolgo mletja v prah«, in pozval k »povzdigovanju« Judov »na častni položaj med narodi zemlje«, tako da bi se jim dovolila vrnitev v deželo Izrael, kjer bi se večina spreobrnila [v krščanstvo](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Conversion_of_the_Jews\_\\(future_event\\)?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Spreobrnitev Judov (prihodnji dogodek)") . [\[ 55 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-56) To bi po Bushovih besedah ​​koristilo ne le Judom, ampak vsemu človeštvu, saj bi ustvarilo »komunikacijsko vez« med človeštvom in Bogom. »Blestelo bo v zloglasju ...«. »To bo sijajna demonstracija resnice vsem rodovom in jezikom.« [\[ 9 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-com-mag-9)

[Herman Melville](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Herman_Melville?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Herman Melville") je to idejo izrazil v pesmi "Clarel; pesem in romanje v Sveti deželi":

> Hebrejski vidci so pravočasno napovedali \
>  vrnitev Jude v njen razcvet; \
>  nekateri kristjani so menili, da je takrat blizu \
>  . Tukaj je bil cilj. Naprej in gor. \
>  S semenom in obdelavo zemlje pomagajte obnoviti – \
>  Pomagajte ponovno vzpostaviti Sveto deželo.

Tajkun [William Eugene Blackstone](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/William_Eugene_Blackstone?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "William Eugene Blackstone") je bil po konferenci navdihnjen za objavo knjige *Jezus prihaja* , ki se je lotila restavratorstva. Njegova knjiga je bila prevedena in objavljena v [jidišu . 24. in 25. novembra 1890 je Blackstone v Prvi metodistični episkopalni cerkvi v ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Yiddish?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Jidiš")[Chicagu](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Chicago?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Chicago") organiziral konferenco o preteklosti, sedanjosti in prihodnosti Izraela, na kateri so sodelovali voditelji številnih krščanskih skupnosti. Sprejete so bile resolucije o sožalju do zatiranih Judov, ki živijo v Rusiji, vendar je bil Blackstone prepričan, da takšne resolucije – čeprav so jih sprejeli ugledni možje – niso zadostne. Odločno se je zavzemal za preselitev Judov v Palestino. Leta 1891 je pri predsedniku [Benjaminu Harrisonu](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Benjamin_Harrison?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Benjamin Harrison") lobiral za obnovo Judov v peticiji, ki jo je podpisalo 413 uglednih Američanov in je postala znana kot [Blackstoneov spomenik](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Blackstone_Memorial?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Spomenik Blackstone") . [\[ 56 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-Merkley-57) Med imeni so bili predsednik vrhovnega sodišča ZDA, predsednik predstavniškega doma, predsednik odbora za zunanje odnose predstavniškega doma in več drugih kongresnikov, Rockefeller, Morgan in znani industrialci. [\[ 56 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-Merkley-57) Med drugim je pisalo: »Zakaj sile, ki so po berlinski pogodbi leta 1878 dale Bolgarijo Bolgarom in Srbijo Srbom, zdaj ne bi vrnile Palestine Judom? ... Te province, pa tudi Romunija, Črna gora in Grčija, so bile odvzete Turkom in dane njihovim naravnim lastnikom. Ali Palestina ne pripada prav tako Judom?« [\[ 57 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-58)



### V Veliki Britaniji

![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/8/8d/Ordecharleswingate.jpg/250px-Ordecharleswingate.jpg)

 V času [mandata v Palestini](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Mandate_Palestine?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Mandatna Palestina") so se Britanci težko spopadali z uravnoteženimi simpatijami do Judov in Arabcev. Nekateri, kot je [Orde Wingate](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Orde_Wingate?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Orde Wingate") , so se borili ob boku [Hagane](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Haganah?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Hagana") kot del [posebnih nočnih enot](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Special_Night_Squads?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Posebne nočne enote") .

Britanski reformatorji so o obnovi Judov pisali že v 16. stoletju, ta ideja pa je imela močno podporo med puritanci. [\[ 7 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-shapira-7) Medtem ko je judovski nacionalizem v začetku 19. stoletja naletel na sovražnost [britanskih Judov](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/British_Jews?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Britanski Judje") , [\[ 58 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-friedman-59) so se ideje, ki so podpirale obnovo Judov v Palestini ali deželi Izrael, v [britanski](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/British_Empire?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Britanski imperij") javni diskurz pojavile v tridesetih letih 19. stoletja. Vsi takšni odnosi niso bili naklonjeni Judom; deloma so jih oblikovala različna [protestantska](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Protestant?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "protestantski") prepričanja [\[ 8 \] ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-lewis-8)[\[ 59 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-60) ali pa meja [filosemitizma](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Philo-Semitism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Filosemitizem") med klasično izobraženo britansko elito. [\[ 60 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-61)

Na pobudo [lorda Shaftesburyja je Britanija leta 1838 v ](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Anthony_Ashley-Cooper,\_7th_Earl_of_Shaftesbury?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Anthony Ashley-Cooper, 7. grof Shaftesburyjski")[Jeruzalemu](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Jerusalem_in_Christianity?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Jeruzalem v krščanstvu") ustanovila konzulat , kar je bil prvi diplomatski sestanek v Palestini. [\[ 8 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-lewis-8)

Leta 1839 je [Škotska cerkev](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Church_of_Scotland?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Škotska cerkev") poslala [Andrewa Bonarja](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Andrew_Bonar?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Andrej Bonar") , [Roberta Murrayja M'Cheyneja](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Robert_Murray_M%27Cheyne?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Robert Murray M'Cheyne") , Alexandra Blacka in [Alexandra Keitha](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Alexander_Keith\_\\(Free_Church_minister\\)?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Alexander Keith (duhovnik svobodne cerkve)") na misijo, da bi poročali o stanju Judov v Palestini. Njihovo poročilo je bilo široko objavljeno. [\[ 61 \]](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Christian_Zionism?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc#cite_note-62) Potovali so skozi Francijo, Grčijo in Egipt, iz Egipta pa po kopnem do [Gaze](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Gaza_City?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Gaza") . Na poti domov so obiskali [Sirijo](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Syria?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Sirija") , [Avstrijsko cesarstvo](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Austrian_Empire?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Avstrijsko cesarstvo") in nekatere nemške države. Poiskali so judovske skupnosti in jih spraševali o njihovi pripravljenosti sprejeti Kristusa, ločeno pa o njihovi pripravljenosti na vrnitev v Izrael, kot je bilo prerokovano v Svetem pismu. [Alexander Keith](https://en-wikipedia-org.translate.goog/wiki/Alexander_Keith\_\\(Free_Church_minister\\)?\_x_tr_sl=en&\_x_tr_tl=sl&\_x_tr_hl=sl&\_x_tr_pto=sc "Alexander Keith (duhovnik svobodne cerkve)") je potovanje opisal v svoji knjigi iz leta 1844 *Dežela Izrael po zavezi z Abrahamom, Izakom in Jakobom* .
